# Skills · Q · part 23

918 entries, question-reframe to quick-fix. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **question-reframe**: Reframe the research question at the right altitude, and state what evidence would actually answer it. ([paulthorson/agentic-governance](https://github.com/paulthorson/agentic-governance/tree/HEAD/adversarial-researcher/skills/question-reframe) · ★4)
- **question-research**: Research one focused question, discover sources, investigate whether an answer already exists, gather public-web evidence or current data, compare conflicting sources, identify evidence gaps, or build an evidence dossier for a human/LLM judgement. Use for iterative, retained, evidence-linked research; skip a simple fact check, one-source lookup, drafting, and multi-topic campaigns. ([stevehalliwell/agent-skills](https://github.com/stevehalliwell/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/question-research))

- **question-review**: Resolve accumulated open decision-questions by dispatching a fresh, independent specialist subagent per question — each grounded in the real primary sources (the actual code, not just the design doc), and, for a genuinely-open question, kept BLIND to the coordinator's own leaning so it forms its own view — then compare each specialist's answer to your own call and decide. Use this whenever open questions or pending decisions have piled up and the principal asks, in their own words, to "dispatch/send subagents to answer the open questions," "get independent takes on these decisions," "resolve… ([AustinHummel/claude-org](https://github.com/AustinHummel/claude-org/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/question-review))
- **question-review**: Review the customer questions a Searcherries project tracks: which ones produce off-topic or brand-free AI answers, how to rephrase them, which to retire, and which new questions the search data suggests. Use when the user asks whether their tracked questions or prompts are right, or which questions to add. ([eduardmur/searcherries-claude-plugin](https://github.com/eduardmur/searcherries-claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/question-review))
- **question-review**: Answer the user's explicit review questions about any artifact — code, stories, documentation, architecture — each with a direct answer, evidence, and a confidence level. Use when: the user brings specific questions to review an artifact against, 'review this and tell me whether…', 'does this handle X?'. DO NOT USE FOR: open-ended improvement ideas (suggestion-review) or a TODO sweep (todo-review). ([JSdotNet/ai-plugins](https://github.com/JSdotNet/ai-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/review/skills/question-review))
- **question-router**: Classify every analytical request (anything about data, metrics, trends, segments, funnels, revenue, retention, experiments, or "why did X change") into complexity levels L1-L5 and route it before any analytical workflow starts, including follow-ups mid-analysis and chart requests. Simple lookups get a direct answer; investigations get the depth they need. Run this first even when the question looks simple. ([ai-analyst-lab/ai-analyst](https://github.com/ai-analyst-lab/ai-analyst/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/question-router) · ★328)

- **questionnaire-agent-broke-parsing-merg-b1355dd3e6fb0a8b**: Fixes this security questionnaire failure. Use when the exact error in the title blocks the workflow it names. Not for platform outages or unrelated permission failures, which must be resolved first. - **questionnaire-agent-loop-reassigning-t-634d1e63b6b93577**: Fixes this security questionnaire failure. Fix: Give each unanswered question a single owning assignee recorded on the question, block reassignment unless the owner releases it with a reason, and stop the run when the same question changes owner twice. Use when the exact error in the title blocks the workflow it names. Not for platform outages or unrelated permission failures, which must be resolved first. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/questionnaire-agent-loop-reassigning-t-634d1e63b6b93577))
